# PT-04: Firm policy recheck (fields a to h)

Run date: 2026-09-25. PT-04 deliverable for `.claude/plans/prop-tools-2026-09-25/`.

## Scope and method

| Field | Question |
| --- | --- |
| (a) | Account caps (eval, funded, live) and pooling across plans, sizes and household |
| (b) | Trigger for moving from sim funded to live (payout count, single-day profit, cumulative payouts, discretion) |
| (c) | Effect of going live on other accounts: sim accounts closed or frozen, new eval purchases blocked, cooldown after a live bust |
| (d) | Inactivity rule: day count, day type, minimum qualifying amount |
| (e) | Conduct rules tied to position sizing or rapid re-buys |
| (f) | Copy trading limits |
| (g) | Dashboard balance convention: nominal (starts at account size) or $0-based |
| (h) | Minimum payout request |

Each firm had two agents. A research agent fetched the firm's own pages (help center, product pages, signed terms) and filled in fields (a) to (h) for every plan the engine models. A verifier agent then re-fetched the cited pages on its own and checked each quote.

Allowed sources were the firm's own live pages, plus user pastes of firm pages where a row says so. Agents did not use memory, the prop-firm skill, third-party sites, the Wayback Machine or browser automation. The `.claude/prop-firms` docs served only as a list of URLs to fetch. Neither agent changed any repo file.

Status values:

- **confirmed:** a firm source states it.
- **conflict:** two firm sources disagree.
- **not found:** no page that loaded states it.
- **needs paste:** the source is blocked or not public.

Notation inside quotes:

- `\|` marks a table-cell boundary on the source page.
- `[dash]` replaces an em dash on the source page, so this file contains no em dashes.
- `[then]` marks where the researcher cut a dash that sat between two verbatim fragments.
- `[...]` marks omitted text.
- ` / ` separates separate quoted fragments.

- `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/apex/ApexTraderFunding.ts`: 50K EOD trailing PA and 50K Intraday trailing PA.
- `ApexLive.ts`: Apex Live.

No field was confirmed. On 2026-09-25, every apextraderfunding.com page returned HTTP 403 from Cloudflare (`server: cloudflare`, cf-ray a40c95e7ff023921-AMS). The agents tried curl with a browser user agent and WebFetch.

## 2. Take Profit Trader (TPT)

Engine plans, in `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/tpt/`:

- 50K Test to PRO: `TakeProfitTrader.ts`
- PRO+: `buildTptLivePlan` in `TptLive.ts`
- PRO+ Development: `buildTptLiveDevelopmentPlan` in `TptLive.ts`

On 2026-09-25 the researcher read all 87 help-center articles through the public Zendesk Help Center API: https://takeprofittraderhelp.zendesk.com/api/v2/help_center/en-us/articles.json returned HTTP 200 on a single page, with next_page null. The article HTML pages themselves return 403. The takeprofittrader.com homepage returned 200 to curl. Every other takeprofittrader.com path returned a Cloudflare 403.

No household cap is stated. ### Conflicts

- **(c) Live exclusivity of the other PRO accounts.** The two sides are quoted in the table above. The Rules for Multiple Accounts line "your full five-account allowance moves with you" fits either reading. It may mean that only the upgraded PRO account is paused, but that is not settled. A paste of the terms or the PRO+ agreement would decide it.

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

1. `minPayoutRequest` $0.01 and the PRO buffer gate match the sources. The $50 charge on withdrawals of $250 or less is a fee, not a minimum.
2. PRO+ dashboards are $0-based, while Test and PRO are nominal.
3. The engine may not model two live-exclusivity economics: the $5,000 PRO profit freeze while PRO+ is active, and the deduction of a negative PRO+ balance from that $5,000.
4. The PRO+ Development article does not state its starting balance or an inactivity rule.
5. The throttle of 10 passed Tests per 30 days is separate from the cap of 5 active funded accounts.

## 3. Tradeify

Engine plans:

- `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/tradeify/Tradeify.ts`: Growth, Lightning, Select Daily, Select Flex.
- `TradeifyLive.ts`: Tradeify Elite Live.

On 2026-09-25, every help.tradeify.co article returned HTTP 403 (Cloudflare) to both curl and WebFetch. The following tradeify.co pages loaded with HTTP 200:

- /funded-trader-agreement: the signed terms, "Last modified: May 2026".
- /: the homepage, including its inline GrowthData, SelectData and LightningData plan config.
- /select-plan and /select-plan-paid-daily.
- The firm's comparison pages.
- /terms-of-use, which has only a single-login rule.

Two help articles were not fetched on 2026-09-25. Their values come from the user's own browser pastes of 2026-09-23:

- 12853966: dateModified 2026-09-10.
- 14369021: dateModified 2026-09-01.

The rows that rest on those pastes are marked. If a same-day fetch is required, downgrade them to needs paste.

### Findings

| Field | Plan | Status | Value | Quote | URL | Fetched |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a | All sim funded plans (Growth, Lightning, Select Daily, Select Flex) | confirmed | At most 5 Simulated Funded Accounts at once. The cap is pooled across ALL funded types (Advanced, Growth, Lightning, Select Flex, Select Daily) and applies per individual AND per household. The homepage plan data (Max Accounts 5) and the /select-plan FAQ ("up to 5 active accounts") agree. The engine sets `MAX_FUNDED_ACCOUNTS` = 5 per plan; whether the engine pools that 5 across plans and household is unchecked. | "4.2 Maximum Number of Simulated Funded Accounts: The Company limits the maximum number of Simulated Funded Accounts that may be held by any single individual to five (5). This limit applies on a per-individual and per-household basis and across ALL funded account types, including Advanced Challenge funded accounts, Growth Challenge funded accounts, Lightning Funded accounts, Select Flex funded accounts, and Select Daily funded accounts." / "Example: A trader may NOT hold three (3) Lightning Funded accounts, two (2) Select Flex accounts, and one (1) Growth funded account. The maximum total is five (5) funded accounts in any combination." | https://tradeify.co/funded-trader-agreement | 2026-09-25 (curl, HTTP 200) |

### Disagreement resolved: Tradeify (e), Select reset cap

The researcher wrote that every Select size's Reset Fee tooltip says "Allowed up to 10 resets per month". On 2026-09-25 the verifier re-fetched https://tradeify.co/ (HTTP 200), and the page shows that this is not true for every size:

> broker_name: 'Tradesea' ... name: '25K' ... ['Reset Fee', '$75', { tooltip: 'Allowed up to 10 resets one time' }] ; Tradesea 50K: ['Reset Fee', '$109', { tooltip: 'Allowed up to 10 resets one time' }]

The quoted "per month" tooltip is present, but only on these entries:

- the Tradovate and WealthCharts Select sizes;
- the Tradesea 100K and 150K entries.

Several other Reset Fee entries ($169, $229, $95, $60) have empty tooltips, which fits the Growth reset cards having no cap tooltip.

Resolution: the 10-reset cap wording depends on the platform, so the "every Select size" wording is corrected in the row above. The row's status stays "not found": no conduct trigger tied to rapid re-buys exists.

### Conflicts

- **(b) Path to live.** The agreement, the homepage and 12853966 say 3 payouts on one account or 10 in total. The stale comparison page says "4 payouts (20 days)". The same stale page also still shows "Up to 5 accounts totaling $1M" and a "$1,500" cap. Recorded as a conflict; the agreement wins.
- Outside (a) to (h), minor: the homepage says Elite live rewards are "up to $18,000 per account", while /tradeify-vs-lucid-trading says "up to $20,000" on a 50K.

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

1. The engine's rolling 7-day inactivity window is stricter than the agreement's "at least one trade per calendar week", and the agreement makes inactivity discretionary.
2. The funded cap of 5 is pooled across all plan types and the household, but each engine plan carries `maxFundedAccounts` 5.
3. Elite Live blocks every other Tradeify account for the trader and the household, and blocks new purchases. The treatment of existing sim accounts and any cooldown after a live bust are not stated.
4. The Select 300K is a separate product with its own rules: max 3 per user, exempt from the 15-evaluation monthly limit, no resets. The engine does not model it.

---

## 4. FundedNext Futures

Engine plans (`FundedNextVariant`, plus `FundedNextLive.ts`): Legacy, Flex, Rapid Pro, Rapid Pro DLL Add-On, Rapid Daily, FNL:003 50K Instant, and FundedNext Live.

Every page was fetched with curl on 2026-09-25 from helpfutures.fundednext.com and fundednext.com, and every cited page returned HTTP 200. The old article id https://helpfutures.fundednext.com/en/articles/10740571 returns 404. It was a stale link, and nothing depends on it.

https://fundednext.com/instant-account-terms covers CFD Instant accounts only, so it was not used for FNL:003.

### Conflicts

- **(b) Flex live trigger.** 15430139 says 15 Performance Rewards. The 14283903 TL;DR says $100K of Total Active Profits or 5 withdrawals from a single Flex Account.
- Outside (a) to (h), the new live pages (16522296, 15900277, 15430139) contradict themselves on the MLL lock:
  - Section 5's example table says "MLL locks at the starting balance if not already locked", a $2,000 floor on 50K.
  - Section 6 says "Your MLL locks $1,000 below your starting balance", a $1,000 floor.
  - The engine follows Section 6.

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

1. Legacy's live path, described only on the "(Former)" page 14283903, differs from the new structure: a $100K trigger, a settlement, reserve and deposit split, and purchases halted while live. The engine keys `LIVE_PLAN_BUILDERS` by FirmId, so Legacy is simulated with the new-structure FundedNext Live plan. Confirm this with the user.
2. The engine does not model:
   - the $750K eval allocation cap;
   - household pooling of the 5 funded accounts;
   - the 10-per-month funded issuance cap;
   - the on-hold queue;
   - live-transition triggers;
   - live cooldowns (2 weeks, or 4+ weeks at discretion);
   - DTP limits (1% risk per trade, $50K new-allocation cap).
3. The engine matches on the 5-account cap, the FNL:003 3-cap, 30-day inactivity, every minimum and maximum payout above, and the $100 live minimum.

### Conflicts

1. **(b) Pro and Direct lifetime payout count.** New Live Structure (updated this week) cites "final payout (Payout 5)", and the Direct table has a "Payouts 4-5" column. The Pro and Direct funded pages say "No simulated payout caps". Only Flex states the 5-payout cap on its own page.
2. **(c) Refund of 0-payout accounts on the move to live.** New Live Structure requires at least 50% of drawdown remaining; the LucidDaily Live page does not.
3. **(h) Flex payouts page is internally inconsistent** on the basis of the maximum: "50% of their account balance" in the text, "50% of Profit" in the table.

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

- These match: `MAX_FUNDED_ACCOUNTS` 5, Flex `maxLifetimePayouts` 5, `INACTIVITY_CLOSURE_DAYS` 30, the $500 minimum for Pro, Flex, Direct and Daily, the Daily $15,000 transition cap, and the $0 live start.
- As far as the plan files show, the engine does not model two effects: all sim accounts closing on the move to live, and the household sim ban while live.

## 6. MyFundedFutures (MFFU)

Engine plans:

- `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/mffu/MyFundedFutures.ts`: Rapid 50K, Rapid EOD 50K, Pro 50K, Builder 50K.
- `MffuRapidLive.ts`: Rapid Live 50K.

All sources were fetched with curl on 2026-09-25, and every page returned HTTP 200:

- the help center (help.myfundedfutures.com);
- the plan pages (myfundedfutures.com/plans/rapid, /rapid-eod, /pro, /builder);
- the Terms (myfundedfutures.com/terms).

The old inactivity article https://help.myfundedfutures.com/en/articles/11972075-inactivity-rule now returns 404; 16596524 replaced it.

### Conflicts

1. **Carried conflict "MFF Rapid EOD 3-cap scope": RESOLVED** by 16498635. The 3 is a plan-specific sub-cap inside the pooled firm-wide cap of 5. The remaining gap is whether EOD 25K and 50K share one 3. The `.claude/prop-firms` open-questions entry can be updated with the quote in the table above.
2. **(a) Builder cap scope across sizes:** one Builder account per user, versus a maximum of 2 on Builder 25K.
3. **(b) Pro live trigger:** automatic after 3 consecutive payouts, or a review; and whether the $20K milestone is a trigger or only a review.
4. **(b) Builder live trigger:** the payout policy's "$100k total sim cap", versus the 5-payout ladder with a $2,000 cap per cycle.
5. Outside (a) to (h):
   - Rapid live contracts: the Rapid EOD plan page LIVE table lists 50K max contracts as "4 / 40", while the Rapid Live help article gives "3 mini / 30 micro". `MffuRapidLive.ts` models 3/30.
   - Builder MLL: the payout policy says "After your first payout, the Max Loss Limit resets to $100", while the Builder article says the MLL locks at $100 above the starting balance. Not verified further.

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

1. `MyFundedFutures.ts` says Rapid has no inactivity rule. That is stale for accounts bought on or after 2026-08-25: 16596524 and Terms s.7 apply a 7-day rule to all eval and sim-funded accounts.
2. `MyFundedFutures.ts` attributes the "no inactivity timer/rule" quote to 13134718. It is actually in 12109396.
3. No household pooling was found. Terms 23.1.3 require every account to belong to "the same verified individual", and device sharing is prohibited (8230018, 8444599). "Household" appears only in the MyFundedClub loyalty and referral rules (16130843).
4. These match the live sources:
   - minimum payouts: Rapid $500, Rapid EOD $500, Pro $1,000, Builder $500, Rapid Live $250;
   - funded caps: Rapid 5, Pro 5, Rapid EOD 3, Builder 1.

## 7. Topstep

Engine plans: every `TopStepVariant` and the LFA in `TopStepLive.ts`. The variants are StandardStandard, StandardConsistency, NoFeeStandard and NoFeeConsistency, their Dll twins, and ProAccount.

All sources were fetched with curl on 2026-09-25: the help.topstep.com articles, and the topstep.com pages terms-of-use, express-funded-account-rules and live-funded-account-rules. All returned HTTP 200. Two guessed URLs, topstep.com/pricing and topstep.com/trading-combine, returned 404; they are not source pages and not blocked.

### Conflicts

1. **(h) XFA per-request cap.** The help center gives per-size caps. topstep.com/express-funded-account-rules (last updated June 26, 2025) gives a flat $5,000 / $6,000 and is stale.
2. **(h) LFA cap basis.** The help center says 50% of account balance. topstep.com/live-funded-account-rules says 50% of the trader's share of Trading Profits and adds a 90% total-payout ceiling.
3. Outside (a) to (h), Capital Expansion timing differs:
   - Help center: "Reviewed every Monday morning", with funds in 1-2 business days.
   - topstep.com LFA rules: profits "measured after market close on Friday", applied "by the following Tuesday".

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

1. The DLL doubled-cap conflict, which `TopStep.ts` marks as unresolved, is now settled by the payout policy FAQ. The higher cap needs the DLL to be added at the original Trading Combine purchase, and the offer is limited-time.
2. `TopStep.ts` sets `maxFundedAccounts` 5 on each plan. The source makes it one pool of 5 per trader profile.
3. The Pro plan config sets no `minPayoutRequest`. Check whether the default equals $125.
4. The topstep.com XFA rules page is stale against the help center, both on the Back2Funded window (7 vs 30 days) and on the payout caps.

## 8. FTMO Futures

Engine plans: Growth 50K and Pro 50K, in `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/ftmo-futures/FtmoFutures.ts` (`FtmoFuturesVariant.Growth` and `.Pro`). No live plan is modeled; there is no `LIVE_PLAN_BUILDERS` entry.

Every source was fetched live with curl on 2026-09-25, around 19:55 UTC, and all returned HTTP 200. The Global Evaluation T&C PDF (terms-and-conditions-3-v5.pdf, dated 31 August 2026) was extracted with pdftotext. The US T&C (terms-and-conditions-4-v5.pdf) was not read, because the Global document applies to a trader resident in the Netherlands.

### Conflicts

1. **Carried conflict "Alpha Qualified cap trigger": STILL OPEN.** The help center (9492088, modified 2026-07-27) says only an active Advanced Qualified limits a user to 3. The Terms (effective July 27, 2026) say an active Standard or Advanced Qualified does. Suggested support question: "If I hold one active Standard Qualified account bought after July 27, 2026, is my total Qualified limit 3 or 5?"
2. **(a) Advanced cap.** The product page says "up to 5 accounts"; the help center and Terms say 3 Advanced Qualified.
3. **(a) Eval cap.** The Terms set no eval cap; the product pages say "up to 5 accounts at the same time" in an eval context. Suggested support question: "Does the product page's 'up to 5 accounts' count Evaluations?"
4. **(b) Live trigger.** The help center gives a discretionary list (5 performance fees on one Qualified Account, significant lifetime fees). The how-it-works page says "+$40,000 in payable balance, or after 5 payout cycles".
5. **(f) Copy trading.** The help center allows copying across the trader's own Alpha accounts; Terms 8.1.5 allow only copying from the trader's own external account into Alpha.
6. Outside the engine's scope: Direct (not modeled) contradicts itself at 50K. The payout policy says "Maximum Withdrawal: $2,000", and the maximum-withdrawal-request article says "$1,500".

### Engine cross-check flags (report only)

- The `minPayoutRequest` values ($200 Zero, $500 Standard, $1,000 Advanced) match.
- Terms Schedule 4 1.2.5: virtual trading on a Qualified account pauses from the withdrawal request until the amount is withdrawn. This is relevant to per-day payout modeling.
- The Terms' Qualified Daily Loss Guard ("2% of account balance") matches the $1,000 figure for 50K on the overview pages.

## 10. E8 Futures

Engine plans, all $50K, in `src/lib/prop-calculator/firms/e8futures/E8Futures.ts`: Signature, ZeroMax80, ZeroMax100, ZeroStarter80 and ZeroStarter100. The site also sells Signature at $25K, $100K and $150K and Zero at $100K and $200K; the engine does not model those sizes.

On 2026-09-25, curl and WebFetch returned 403 for every helpfutures.e8markets.com and help.e8markets.com URL, and for the e8x.e8markets.com checkout and dashboard. These sit behind E8's "Security check" bot challenge. The pages that loaded with HTTP 200:

- e8futures.com/
- /e8-zero
- /how-it-works
- /compare-simfi-funded-accounts
- /e8-markets-terms-and-conditions (Terms of Service, effective 11/03/2025)

/e8-signature, /e8-one, /payouts and /faq return 404.

### Conflicts

None found. Neither carried open conflict applies.

### Unreachable controlling document

The terms incorporate an "Educational Simulation Participant Agreement (ESPA)", which prevails where it conflicts with the terms. It is available only inside the platform and may hold the cap, live and inactivity rules. The user should paste it if they can reach it.

The account caps the engine already uses (Signature 5, Zero 3) and Signature's $125 minimum payout come from earlier user pastes and could not be re-verified in this run.

### Internal inconsistencies outside fields (a) to (h), or outside the modeled plans

- **FundedNext new live pages:** Section 5 says "MLL locks at the starting balance", while Section 6 says "locks $1,000 below your starting balance". The engine follows Section 6.
- **Lucid Flex payouts:** the text says "50% of their account balance", while the table says "50% of Profit up to $2,000".
- **MFFU Rapid 50K live contracts:** the Rapid EOD plan page says "4 / 40", while the Rapid Live article says "3 mini / 30 micro". `MffuRapidLive.ts` models 3/30.
- **MFFU Builder MLL:** the payout policy says "resets to $100" after the first payout, while the Builder article says it locks at $100 above the start.
- **Topstep Capital Expansion timing:** the help center says Monday review, while the topstep.com LFA rules say Friday close, applied by Tuesday.

#### Flags (FundedNext)

- **Engine flag:** `LIVE_PLAN_BUILDERS` keys FundedNext by `FirmId`, so Legacy currently receives the new-structure builder. Legacy should be NotModeled.
- **(b) Flex conflict widened:** 14878751 (modified 2026-09-25), the Flex product page and the 14283903 TL;DR give a 5-from-one-account trigger and point Flex at the older program with a $10,000 cumulative deposit cap. 15430139 and 16522296 give 15 total rewards and the new structure.
- **Coverage gap:** no page covers a Rapid Pro or Rapid Daily account bought on or before July 10.

## PT-35 encode table (step 0, re-fetched live 2026-09-26)

Run date: 2026-09-26. This is PT-35 step 0. Before the policy data is encoded, every firm's own pages were fetched again for the five PT-35 components: caps, pools and throttles; live triggers; exclusivity and cooldown; inactivity; conduct. Per firm, one research agent fetched the pages and one verifier agent re-fetched the cited pages on its own. Neither agent changed a repo file. The status values and quote notation from the top of this file apply, with one addition:

- **changed since 2026-09-25:** a firm source states it, and the text is new or changed after the 2026-09-25 recheck. PT-35 step 0 requires a second agent to re-verify these rows.

Source kinds: **LiveFetch** (curl or the firm's public API, HTTP 200 on 2026-09-26), **user paste 2026-09-23** (Apex only; not a live fetch), **none** (the live fetch was blocked).

Changed rows are counted only in the "Changed since 2026-09-25" column, not also under Confirmed. Verifier checks are grouped (one check can cover several rows), so the verifier totals do not equal the row totals.

### Values changed since 2026-09-25

Only Take Profit Trader has changed text. All four changed rows come from one new help article, "PRO+ Development: 30-Day Live-Capital Cooldown" (id 39331980656925, created 2026-09-22T11:50:19Z, updated 2026-09-26T07:01:28Z). The Zendesk API now lists 88 articles; on 2026-09-25 it listed 87 and this one was not among them. The TPT verifier re-verified all four rows as a second agent and agreed with each.

1. **TPT live trigger, 50K Test to PRO+ Development:** a trader in the Development cooldown can activate passed Tests straight into PRO+ Development accounts. The verifier notes this is a narrow path (only for traders already in the Development cooldown), not a general Test-to-Development route. The R3 (i) mapping did not know about it.
2. **TPT exclusivity-cooldown, PRO+ Development:** a new loss-triggered cooldown. A $10,000 net loss of TPT live capital across PRO+ Development accounts in any rolling 30 days pauses new purchases for 30 days, and purchasing reopens after a risk-team review. PT-35's `LiveBustCooldown` subclasses cannot express a dollar-threshold, rolling-window, purchase-only cooldown.
3. **TPT exclusivity-cooldown, trader in cooldown:** EvalPurchaseEffect Blocked (new subscriptions, Test resets, PRO resets) and no standard PRO+ during the cooldown. Existing accounts keep trading. A breach counts the account's actual ending balance, not its loss limit.
4. **TPT conduct, PRO+ Development:** the cooldown is framed as a sustainable-trading rule. Store it as policy data, not as a `ConductCategory`.

The verifier's one caveat: the article was created on 2026-09-22, before the recheck, so "not in the set on 2026-09-25" presumably means it was unpublished or a draft then. The verifier could not check that independently.

No other firm has a page changed since 2026-09-25 (every dateModified, modified_time or "Last updated" matches the recheck, or is older).

### Disagreements

No verifier returned "disagree" on any of the 156 checks. The 10 "could not fetch" verdicts are:

- **Apex (9):** the Apex Live FAQ still returns 403 (verifier cf-ray a411ba0eafd8c886-AMS). Every Apex confirmed and not-found row quotes the user paste of 2026-09-23. The verifier matched all quotes word for word against that paste, but none is confirmed as current.
- **Lucid (1):** https://lucidtrading.com/terms-of-use/ still returns 403 (cf-mitigated: challenge). The row stays needs paste.

Verifier coverage gaps (rows or sources a verifier did not re-fetch itself): Tradeify 11083796, 10495932, 13196343, 17069653 and 10468276 (so the Tradeify discretionary-trigger and rapid-rebuy rows were not independently checked); MFFU 10101257; Topstep ToU s.3 and s.16, the prohibited-strategies news line and the "cannot decline" FAQ; Lucid 15 of the 59 help articles only (so the Lucid throttle and re-buy not-found rows are unverified by the verifier); E8 9453469 (copy trading); FTMO and Alpha engine-side claims (page text only).

### New findings on unchanged pages

These are not page changes. They are values newly recorded, reclassified or resolved in this run from text that was already there on 2026-09-25.

- **Tradeify:** new conflict on the outcome of a missed inactivity week (Common FAQs says the account is deleted after an email warning; the agreement and Guidelines say "may result in" inactive). Growth daily activation throttle (5 per UTC day) newly recorded. The eval purchase throttle is narrowed to Select only, 15 per 30-day window. Four needs-paste rows from PT-04 are resolved through intercom.help: (a) concurrent eval cap, (c) Elite Live cooldown, (f) copy trading, (g) dashboard balance. Article 14369021 is now live-fetched, so the "user paste of 2026-09-23" qualifier can be dropped.
- **Lucid:** the Daily $8,000 "automatic" flag is reclassified from confirmed to conflict (Daily Payouts says automatic, Daily Live says review pool at the risk team's discretion; both pages dateModified 2026-07-27). PT-35 step 4 says "Daily $8,000 automatic", so this needs a decision before encoding `automatic: true`. Flex funded scaling circumvention and the Daily funded red-folder news hard breach are newly recorded. Maxx inactivity is flagged: the engine's Maxx plan sets no `maxConsecutiveIdleDays`.
- **MFFU:** the Pro $20,000 milestone is split out of the Pro conflict as a confirmed review-only trigger. Rapid 50K inactivity mismatch flag: the firm says 7 calendar days (accounts bought on or after 2026-08-25), but the engine's Rapid 50K sets none and calls Rapid exempt (Q15). The engine's live no-inactivity note cites 13134718, but the quote is in 12109396.
- **Topstep:** attribution correction (the "goes on hold" FAQ is on 8284215, not 8284217). The verifier found that the help center settles the Back2Funded conflict: "On May 29th, 2026, the Reactivation window increased from 7 days to 30 days!", so the topstep.com XFA rules page (last updated 2025-06-26) is stale and 30 days is the value to use. Engine flag: `buildPlan` does not set `evalMaxConsecutiveIdleDays` null, so the 30-day funded limit also applies to the Combine (inert at idleDayProbability 0).
- **FTMO Futures:** the sentence "A Live Funded Account is not granted based on any past trading track record." is on the become-a-trader FAQ, not the eligibility FAQ.
- **Alpha Futures:** new microscalping definition conflict (help center: under 10 ticks AND under 2 minutes; Terms Schedule 5: under 10 ticks). Inactivity mismatch flag: the firm says 10 trading days, the engine's three Alpha plans set no `maxConsecutiveIdleDays`.
- **E8 Futures:** the PT-04 needs-paste rows (a), (d), (e) and the Signature (i) row are resolved by reading the same articles at `intercom.help/E8futures` (see the host question below). New conflict on Signature inactivity (7 days vs 60 days). The engine note in E8Futures.ts cites article 10253631, which now returns 404; the live inactivity source is 9453425. The Signature source article 11864618 is 404; the current one is 11755943.
- **FundedNext:** no new finding. 14878751 was modified 2026-09-25T11:09:25Z, the same day as R3, and every R3 quote is still verbatim.

For Tradeify this host was already accepted on 2026-09-25. For E8 the orchestrator must confirm that `intercom.help/E8futures` counts as E8's own help center before the E8 confirmed rows are trusted.
